For a new test set, involving randomly sampled geomodels and control variables, the surrogate achieves a median saturation MAE of 0.028 and median relative errors of 0.2-5% for the other quantities of interest. Importantly, it captures the switch from rate to bottom-hole-pressure control. The surrogate model is used within a hierarchical Markov chain Monte Carlo data assimilation procedure for a synthetic true model under three operational strategies. Substantial uncertainty reduction is achieved for key metaparameters, particularly the fault permeabilities. Posterior predictions for saturation footprints and total injected and mobile CO2 mass are also shown to be generally consistent with true model results.

</details>


### [131] [LLMs Can Annotate Attribution Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02632)
*Ameen Patel,Max Zhang,Nathan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化的电路追踪流水线，通过让语言模型直接对特征描述进行分组来生成supernodes，消除了人工手动分组的繁琐步骤。实验证明该方法生成的supernodes与人工标注同样可解释，并能在两跳Capitals任务中恢复中间跳对应的supernode，同时展示了在开放探索场景中的初步应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路追踪是揭示语言模型内部计算机制的重要技术，但现有的电路追踪方法需要耗费大量时间进行人工手动分组，将单个特征或MLP神经元归入supernodes。这种手动步骤成为电路追踪大规模应用的瓶颈，因此需要自动化方案。

Method: 提出了一个简单的自动化流水线：直接将特征描述输入给一个语言模型，由模型自动将特征分组为supernodes。使用自动化可解释性指标验证生成supernodes的质量，并在两跳Capitals任务和开放探索场景（自动标注1000个维基百科提示补全的归因图）中进行评估。

Result: 生成的supernodes与人工标注的一样可解释；在两跳Capitals任务的100个提示中，有97个成功恢复了对应中间跳的supernode；还展示了开放探索场景的初步概念验证，能够自动标注归因图并用LLM判断标记出值得人工审查的图。

Conclusion: 该工作表明即使是简单的自动化也能产生有意义的归因图标注，为自动化的电路追踪研究奠定了基础，并鼓励更多相关工作。

Abstract: Circuit tracing is an exciting technique for revealing the internal computation of language models, but it requires a time-intensive manual step of grouping individual features or MLP neurons into supernodes. We present a simple pipeline for automating this step: directly presenting feature descriptions to a language model that groups them into supernodes. Using automated interpretability metrics, we confirm that supernodes generated by our pipeline are as interpretable as those generated by human annotators. On a two-hop Capitals task, our pipeline recovers a supernode corresponding to the intermediate hop in 97 of 100 prompts. Finally, we present a simple proof of concept using our pipeline for open-ended exploration, where we automatically annotate 1000 attribution graphs from Wikipedia prompt completions and then use an LLM judge to flag interesting graphs worth human review. We hope this work demonstrates that even simple automation can produce meaningful attribution graph annotations, motivating further work on automated circuit tracing.

</details>


### [132] [Verifier-Guided Model Discovery for Physical Dynamical Systems with Pretrained Symbolic Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02662)
*Farbod Faraji,Francesco Belardinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种验证器引导（VG）的神经到符号工作流，以ODEFormer为符号骨干，利用动力学和物理可接受性准则从多轨迹候选方程池中筛选方程，实现向高维物理数据的可靠转移。在Van der Pol振荡器和涡脱落现象上验证了该方法，能发现可解释的降阶方程并泛化到未见参数域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真物理仿真成本过高，机器学习替代模型不透明且隐含系统动力学假设、泛化性有限。预训练的变换器虽然可以将合成ODE轨迹映射为方程，但在高维物理数据上可靠转移仍然是一个开放挑战。

Method: 开发验证器引导（VG）工作流，以ODEFormer为符号骨干，通过动力学和物理可接受性准则从多轨迹候选方程池中筛选最佳方程，实现可靠转移。结合坐标约简和符号发现处理高维数据，针对固定和变化的雷诺数进行涡脱落的降阶方程发现。

Result: 在Van der Pol振荡器上，VG优于原始ODEFormer工作流。在涡脱落问题上，VG发现了固定参数的降阶方程，能恢复基本脱落振荡器和高阶谐波，无需特定尾迹候选库或预设Navier-Stokes结构；跨参数模型能泛化到保留的流动区域。此外发现仅靠重建保真度不能决定符号可发现性，潜动力学与骨干预训练分布的兼容性至关重要。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了验证器引导的神经到符号方法论，为自然科学中可解释、可物理审计的预测提供了有效途径，并强调了潜动力学与预训练分布兼容性的重要性。

Abstract: Reliable forecasting of nonlinear physical systems underpins scientific discovery and engineering decision-making. Yet high-fidelity simulations are prohibitively costly, and machine-learning surrogates can be opaque and encode assumptions about system dynamics, limiting generalizability. Pretrained transformers mapping synthetic ODE trajectories to equations offer interpretable alternatives, promising transfer without system-specific equation knowledge. Transferring them reliably to high-dimensional physical data, however, remains an open challenge. We develop a verifier-guided (VG) workflow around ODEFormer as a symbolic backbone, using dynamical and physical-admissibility criteria to select from a multi-trajectory candidate equation pool, enabling transfer. On canonical Van der Pol oscillators, VG outperforms the original ODEFormer workflow across held-out initial conditions. We then address vortex shedding, a phenomenon occurring in atmospheric and plasma systems of societal relevance, through coordinate reduction and symbolic discovery at fixed and varying Reynolds numbers. VG discovers fixed-parameter reduced-order equations that recover the fundamental shedding oscillator and higher harmonics without a wake-specific candidate library or prescribed Navier-Stokes structure, while the cross-parameter model generalizes to withheld regimes. Reconstruction fidelity alone did not determine symbolic discoverability, highlighting the importance of compatibility between latent dynamics and the backbone's pretraining distribution. This work establishes a verifier-guided neural-to-symbolic methodology for interpretable and physically auditable forecasting in the natural sciences.

</details>


### [133] [CT-HEG: A Bidirectional, Timestamp-Attributed Event Graph for ICU In-Hospital Mortality Prediction - An Architectural Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02663)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin,Rahnuma Tabassum Orpita,Asaduzzaman Anik,Eklachur Rahman Bhuiyan,Marjahan Risalat,SM Wali Ullah,Asif Ahamed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CT-HEG（连续时间异构电子健康记录图谱）模式，将ICU住院过程建模为带类型的、带时间戳的图（含visit、vital、lab_event三种节点类型及2D边属性），并实例化为CHIRP-Net（四层异质GATv2Conv网络），在MIMIC-IV v3.1上（31,142次ICU住院）实现5种子平均AUROC 0.8449，集成后AUROC 0.8618。消融研究发现：双向连通性是模型有效工作的关键前提（移除后将导致AUROC下降0.1968），时间注意力边特征贡献0.0247 AUROC，而将所有异质边类型合并为单一关系（参数减少7倍）反而在所有种子上优于完整模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型能处理不规则采样但忽略了异质实体间的类型化关系结构，而现有图模型假设固定间隔输入。ICU死亡率预测需要同时处理不规则时间观测和异质实体类型，因此需要一种既能建模不规则时序又能利用类型化关系结构的统一框架。

Method: 提出CT-HEG模式：每个ICU住院编码为一个带类型、带时间戳的图，包含三类节点（visit、vital、lab_event）和2D边属性（t_hours/48标准化时间差，value_norm标准化值），无需插值。实例化为CHIRP-Net，即四层异质GATv2Conv网络，在MIMIC-IV v3.1上使用5个随机种子和bootstrap置信区间进行评估，并与逻辑回归、mTAND、Transformer和GRU-D基线比较，同时进行消融研究。

Result: CHIRP-Net达到5种子平均AUROC 0.8449±0.0071（AUPRC 0.4958±0.0209），集成后AUROC 0.8618（95% CI: 0.8485-0.8745），校准后ECE为0.0307。消融发现：移除反向边会使AUROC下降0.1968±0.0073（说明双向连通性至关重要）；时间注意力边特征贡献0.0247±0.0093 AUROC；将异质边类型合并为单一关系（参数减少7倍）在所有种子上反而优于完整模型（对异质性的必要性提出了质疑）。

Conclusion: CT-HEG支持对不规则EHR数据进行建模且校准效果合理（经验证集拟合的温度缩放后），但外部验证、预先指定的时间评估和人口统计学公平性审计仍是必要的，在声称稳健性之前必须完成这些工作。

Abstract: Accurate ICU mortality prediction requires modeling irregular clinical observations across heterogeneous entity types. Existing sequence models handle irregular sampling but ignore typed relational structure; existing graph models assume fixed-interval inputs. We introduce the Continuous-Time Heterogeneous EHR Graph (CT-HEG) schema and evaluate which architectural choices drive predictive performance. CT-HEG encodes each ICU stay as a typed, timestamped graph with three node types (visit, vital, lab_event) and 2D edge attributes (t_hours/48, value_norm) encoding timing and value without imputation. We instantiate CT-HEG as CHIRP-Net, a four-layer heterogeneous GATv2Conv network, evaluated on MIMIC-IV v3.1 (31,142 ICU stays, LOS>=48h, 13.4% mortality) with five seeds and bootstrapped confidence intervals, against logistic regression, mTAND, a Transformer, and GRU-D, plus an ablation study. CHIRP-Net achieved 5-seed mean AUROC 0.8449+/-0.0071 (AUPRC 0.4958+/-0.0209); the ensemble achieved AUROC 0.8618 (95% CI: 0.8485-0.8745). Removing reverse edges disconnected observation nodes from the visit readout, cutting AUROC by 0.1968+/-0.0073. Time-attentive edge features contributed 0.0247+/-0.0093 AUROC. Collapsing heterogeneous edge types into one relation (7x fewer parameters) outperformed the full model on all seeds. Post-calibration ECE was 0.0307. Temporal and demographic subgroup analyses were explored but not reported here, pending follow-up work. Bidirectional connectivity was necessary for the model to use its inputs at all, and CT-HEG was reasonably well calibrated after validation-fitted temperature scaling. These results support CT-HEG for irregular EHR data, while external validation, a pre-specified temporal evaluation, and a demographic fairness audit remain necessary before any claim of robustness. Code: https://github.com/nasiruddinstudents-ctrl/chirp-net-mimic-iv.

</details>


### [134] [Sphere Retraction Normalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02668)
*Jie Zhang,Cheng-Fang Su,Yi-Jui Huang,Min-Te Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了p-SpheretNorm，一个参数化的角度收缩（angular retraction）统一框架，将欧氏残差连接与GeoNorm置于同一理论体系。实验表明，在纳米GPT上，有限p值的角度收缩方法优于GeoNorm等现有方案，说明指数映射并非球面残差流的最优选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GeoNorm通过黎曼指数映射在超球面上正交化层输出，但指数映射只是众多retraction映射族中的一员。作者希望探究：其他retraction映射是否能带来更好的性能，以及是否存在一个统一的框架来理解所有球形残差连接方法。

Method: 作者证明在超球面上，整个retraction映射族可简化为单一标量设计选择，即如何将更新幅度转换为旋转角。基于此提出p-SpheretNorm，一个单参数角度收缩族，旋转角饱和而非无界增长。p=1和p=2分别对应Proj-SpheretNorm（度量投影收缩）和Cay-SpheretNorm（Cayley收缩），而恒等映射和GeoNorm分别作为两个极限端点出现。

Result: 在nanoGPT上，所有三种方法（p=1,2及有限p值）均优于现有的轻量级深度连接方案。最佳验证损失在有限p值处取得，表明指数映射不是球面残差流的首选收缩方式。

Conclusion: 球形残差流的最优收缩并非指数映射（GeoNorm），而是一个有限p值的角度收缩，这为设计深度网络连接机制开辟了新的设计空间。

Abstract: Residual connections are the de facto mechanism for training deep neural networks stably. Geodesic Normalization (GeoNorm) recasts them on a Riemannian manifold, orthogonalizing each layer output against the current hidden state and applying the resulting update through the Riemannian exponential map. Every hidden state thus keeps a constant $\ell_{2}$-norm, confining the residual stream to a hypersphere. The exponential map, however, is only one member of a broad family of retraction maps. We show that on the hypersphere this entire family collapses to a single scalar design choice. What distinguishes one retraction from another is only how the magnitude of an update is converted into a rotation angle within the plane spanned by the hidden state and the update. This view places Euclidean residual connections and GeoNorm in one framework. Instantiating it with the metric projection retraction and the Cayley retraction yields Proj-SpheretNorm and Cay-SpheretNorm, which are exactly norm-preserving yet require only algebraic operations. Both prove to be members of a one-parameter family of angular retractions, $p$-SpheretNorm, whose rotation angle saturates rather than growing without bound. The two methods above are recovered exactly at $p = 1$ and $p = 2$, while the identity map and GeoNorm arise only as limits at either end. On nanoGPT, all three methods outperform existing lightweight deep connection schemes, and the best validation loss is attained at finite $p$, indicating that the exponential map is not the preferred retraction for spherical residual streams but merely one end of a spectrum.

</details>


### [135] [Learning Molecular Representations from Cellular Phenotypes with Structure Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02688)
*Xuan Lin,Jingyu Sheng,Tengfei Ma,Li Sun,Dapeng Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PhenMol是一个面向表型药物发现的、保持化学空间结构的分子表示学习框架。它通过将分子和细胞表征解耦为共享与私有组件，在表型引导对齐的同时保留分子邻域组织，在约3.04×10⁴个分子-细胞形态对上提升了分子性质预测（270项生物活性任务）、分子-表型检索和临床试验结果预测，并显著降低了嵌入失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态表示学习方法在优化跨模态对齐时，未考虑化学空间的内在组织，导致分子表示发生扭曲并丢失结构信息。作者旨在解决这一缺陷，在整合细胞表型信息的同时保留分子的化学结构邻域关系。

Method: PhenMol将分子和细胞表示分别解耦为共享组件（用于跨模态对齐）和私有组件（用于保留各自模态的独特信息）。其中，专门设计的分子分支负责保留化学结构组织，使表型引导的对齐不会干扰分子邻域结构，从而实现结构保持的表型感知分子表示学习。

Result: 在约3.04×10⁴对分子-细胞形态数据上，PhenMol在270项生物活性任务的分子性质预测、分子-表型检索和临床试验结果预测中均超越现有方法。ECFP4结构分析表明，PhenMol比现有多模态对齐方法更有效地保持了分子邻域并降低了嵌入扭曲。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在多模态分子表示学习中引入结构保持约束至关重要。PhenMol提供了一种有效的范式，将细胞表型与化学知识整合以支持药物发现，凸显了保留化学空间组织在跨模态学习中的价值。

Abstract: Phenotypic drug discovery enables the discovery of functional relationships between molecular structures and cellular responses. However, existing multimodal representation learning methods often optimize cross-modal alignment without considering the intrinsic organization of chemical space, resulting in distorted molecular representations and loss of structural information. We propose \textbf{PhenMol}, a structure-preserving framework for phenotype-aware molecular representation learning. PhenMol disentangles molecular and cellular representations into shared and private components, enabling phenotype-guided alignment while preserving chemical structures through a dedicated molecular branch. This design integrates cellular phenotype information without disrupting molecular neighborhood organization. Experiments on approximately $3.04 \times 10^{4}$ molecule--cell morphology pairs demonstrate that PhenMol improves molecular property prediction across 270 bioactivity tasks, molecule--phenotype retrieval, and clinical trial outcome prediction. Moreover, ECFP4-based structural analysis shows that PhenMol better preserves molecular neighborhoods and reduces embedding distortion compared with existing multimodal alignment methods. These results highlight the importance of structure-aware constraints in multimodal molecular representation learning and provide an effective approach for integrating cellular phenotypes with chemical knowledge for drug discovery.

</details>


### [136] [GLOBE: Trajectory-Aligned Gradient Matching with Structured SparseOptimization for Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02690)
*Hetian Liu,Jin Cui,Mengcheng Shi,Yanbin Hu,Xinyue Long,Boran Zhao,Pengju Pen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GLOBE是一个轨迹对齐的核集选择框架，通过多检查点梯度轨迹表示每个样本，结合多阶矩匹配目标与Group LASSO/Elastic Net结构化稀疏约束，在六个基准和五个架构上持续优于现有核集选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度的核集选择方法通常在单一模型快照上计算梯度，并采用贪心或追踪式选择策略，无法捕捉深度神经网络训练中伴随优化动态演变的样本影响力，也难以处理强相关的样本。这限制了核集选择在下游测试精度上的表现。

Method: GLOBE将每个样本用跨多个训练检查点构建的梯度轨迹表示，以捕捉其在优化不同阶段的贡献。通过多阶匹配目标同时对齐一阶均值与投影非中心二阶矩；结合Group LASSO、Elastic Net正则化和非负预算约束诱导组级与样本级稀疏性并稳定相关轨迹权重；最后采用类别平衡的Top-K选择保证小预算下的类别覆盖。

Result: 在六个基准数据集和五个评估架构上，GLOBE在下游测试准确率上一致优于现有核集选择方法，尤其在低保留率下表现突出，验证了动态梯度信息、多阶分布匹配与结构化稀疏相结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 将动态梯度信息、多阶分布匹配与结构化稀疏相结合是数据高效学习的有效途径，GLOBE为设备端深度神经网络训练的核集选择提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: On-device training of deep neural networks is fundamentally constrained by the computational and memory costs of large-scale datasets. Coreset selection offers a practical solution by retaining only a compact subset of real training samples. However, existing gradient-based methods commonly rely on gradients computed at a single model snapshot and employ greedy or pursuit-based selection procedures, limiting their ability to capture evolving optimization dynamics and handle strongly correlated samples. We propose GLOBE (Gradient Local-Balanced Extraction), a trajectory-aligned coreset selection framework that formulates sample selection as a globally optimized sparse weighting problem. GLOBE represents each sample by a gradient trajectory constructed across multiple training checkpoints, thereby capturing its influence throughout different stages of optimization. To preserve the training behavior of the full dataset, we introduce a multi-order matching objective that jointly aligns the first-order mean and projected uncentered second-order moments of gradient trajectories. GLOBE further combines Group LASSO, Elastic Net regularization, and nonnegative budget constraints to induce group- and sample-level sparsity while stabilizing the weights of correlated trajectories. Finally, class-balanced Top-K selection maintains adequate category coverage under limited sampling budgets. Experiments across six benchmarks and five evaluation architectures demonstrate that GLOBE consistently outperforms existing coreset selection methods in downstream test accuracy, particularly at low retention ratios. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining dynamic gradient information, multi-order distribution matching, and structured sparsity for data-efficient learning.

</details>


### [137] [Output-Aware Rotation for INT2 KV-Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02691)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Woosang Lim,Minsoo Cheong,Sunwoo Lee,Murali Annavaram,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy,Sungjoo Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OptR是一种输出感知的旋转方法，用于低比特KV缓存量化。它通过最小化经过输出投影W_O后的注意力输出误差，而非传统方法仅优化缓存统计量或代理误差，从而在INT2量化下显著提升长上下文LLM推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存已成为长上下文大语言模型推理中的主要内存和带宽瓶颈，超低比特量化（INT2）日益重要。然而，现有基于旋转的INT2方法在完整注意力读出之前优化缓存统计量或代理误差，忽略了模型最终受注意力传播和输出投影W_O误差影响的事实，导致优化目标与真实损失不匹配。

Method: OptR将经过W_O后的注意力输出误差分解为键引起和值引起的两项，并通过完整的INT2量化和注意力路径学习每头正交矫正（per-head orthogonal corrections）。此外，OptR应用注意力等价的键重参数化（attention-equivalent key reparameterization）来减小大的通道偏移（channel-wise offsets），同时不改变softmax分布。

Result: 在三个模型和五个推理及编码基准上，OptR一致地改进了QuaRot和OSCAR两个基线方法，增强了长上下文检索能力，同时保持了分页KV缓存格式且推理开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 输出感知的旋转优化策略能够有效弥补传统方法中优化目标与真实损失之间的错配，在KV缓存极低位量化中取得更优的精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache has become a major memory and bandwidth bottleneck in long-context large language model inference, making ultra-low-bit quantization increasingly important. However, existing rotation-based INT2 methods optimize cache statistics or proxy errors before the complete attention readout, even though the model is ultimately affected by the error propagated through attention and the output projection $W_O$. To address this mismatch, we propose \textit{OptR}, an output-aware rotation method that minimizes post-$W_O$ attention-output error. OptR decomposes the post-$W_O$ attention-output error into key- and value-induced terms and learns per-head orthogonal corrections through the full INT2 quantization and attention path. OptR further applies an attention-equivalent key reparameterization to reduce large channel-wise offsets without changing the softmax distribution. Across three models and five reasoning and coding benchmarks, OptR consistently improves both QuaRot and OSCAR and strengthens long-context retrieval, while preserving the paged KV-cache format with negligible inference overhead.

</details>


### [138] [Measuring Explainer Stability via Attribution Separability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02697)
*Eddie Conti,Álvaro Parafita,Axel Brando*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于分布的框架来捕捉归因方法（AMs）的稳定性，能够评估排序归因向量的可分性程度，并找到特征排序保持可靠的最大索引。该框架还可扩展到数据集层面，用于比较不同归因方法在排名鲁棒性上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归因方法由于定义中的随机成分，可能产生不稳定的归因分数，但缺乏一个系统性的框架来量化这种不稳定性。

Method: 提出基于分布的稳定性分析框架：通过分析归因分数的分布特性来理解排序归因向量的可分性，确定特征排序可靠的最大索引；并将该框架扩展到数据集层面比较不同AMs的排名鲁棒性。

Result: 实验展示了如何将该方法应用于评估解释器的稳定性，该框架提供了评估AMs稳定性的补充准则。

Conclusion: 该框架为评价归因方法的稳定性提供了一种互补的标准，帮助理解在多大程度上特征排序是可靠的。

Abstract: Attribution methods (AMs) assign an importance score to each feature and are widely adopted to explain black-box models. However, most methods can produce variable attribution scores due to stochastic components in their definition. In this paper, we propose a distribution-based framework to capture the stability of attribution scores. In particular, our approach allows to understand the degree of separability in the ranked attribution vector and obtain the largest index for which a feature ranking remains reliable. We further extend this framework to compare AMs based on the robustness of their rankings across a dataset. Through experiments, we demonstrate how to apply our method to evaluate explainer stability. Overall, our approach provides a complementary criterion for evaluating the stability of AMs.

</details>


### [139] [NANQ: Noise-Floor-Aware Mixed-Precision Non-Uniform Quantization for Analog Compute-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02700)
*Yizhe Chen,Wenshuai Yao,Saiya Wang,Yuannuo Feng,Wenbo Qi,Kechao Tang,Ngai Wong,Wenyong Zhou,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NANQ，一个面向模拟CIM的噪声感知混合精度非均匀量化框架，根据eFlash CIM阵列实测响应建模幅度相关权重噪声，自适应分配量化密度和逐层位宽，在2-bit权重量化下显著提升视觉与语言模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CIM场景下的量化方法主要最小化理想量化误差，忽略了硬件噪声底（noise floor），导致精度分配效率低下，无法在器件变化和读取噪声下有效保护低比特量化模型的性能。

Method: NANQ框架包含两部分：(1) 从eFlash CIM阵列实测响应中建模幅度相关的权重噪声，将噪声分布转化为自适应的量化密度，在低噪声区域分配更细的分辨率，在噪声主导区域避免无效精度；(2) 利用统一阈值识别每层在硬件噪声下的精度饱和点，据此分配逐层位宽。

Result: 在eFlash CIM SoC上片上实验表明，在2-bit权重幅度量化下，NANQ相比PowerQuant在视觉模型上准确率提升8.05个百分点，语言模型PPL平均降低54.7%。混合精度NANQ仅用3.2-3.8等效比特即可获取额外量化资源带来的大部分收益。

Conclusion: NANQ通过将硬件噪声感知引入量化设计，实现了高效的混合精度非均匀量化，在模拟CIM平台上以较低比特资源获得接近更高比特量化的性能，验证了硬件感知量化策略对模拟存算系统的有效性。

Abstract: Analog compute-in-memory (CIM) enables energy-efficient neural network inference, but device variation and read noise can severely degrade low-bit quantized models. Existing CIM-oriented quantization methods mainly minimize ideal quantization error, ignoring the hardware noise floor and thus causing inefficient precision allocation. We propose NANQ, a noise-aware mixed-precision non-uniform quantization framework for analog CIM. NANQ models magnitude-dependent weight noise from measured responses of an eFlash CIM array and converts the noise profile into an adaptive quantization density, assigning finer resolution to low-noise regions while avoiding ineffective precision in noise-dominated regions. It further assigns layer-wise bit-widths by identifying each layer's precision saturation point under hardware noise using a unified threshold. On-chip experiments on an eFlash CIM SoC show that, under 2-bit weight-magnitude quantization, NANQ improves vision-model accuracy by 8.05 percentage points and reduces language-model PPL by 54.7% on average over PowerQuant. Mixed-precision NANQ captures most of the gains obtainable from additional quantization resources with only 3.2-3.8 equivalent bits.

</details>


### [140] [Can Training Logs Make Model Comparisons More Precise?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02705)
*Wei-Jung Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了如何利用随机训练（stochastic training）过程中的训练日志作为协变量，对多次运行的模型性能比较进行更精确的估计。作者提出臂特定协变量调整（arm-specific covariate adjustment）方法，即每个模型仅使用自身运行的统计量进行调整，原始均值差仍作为报告的效应量。实验覆盖三个架构和三个数据集的视觉任务，发现基于早期训练日志的简单调整常能降低模型比较的不确定性，但主要的局限在于协变量选择——广泛搜索日志池中最相关的统计量反而会引入更多噪声而非减少噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较随机训练的模型需要从多次重复运行中估计性能差异及其不确定性。传统的均值比较方法忽略了训练过程中蕴含的丰富信息，作者希望探索能否利用训练日志中的协变量来提高模型比较的精度，从而在有限计算资源下获得更可靠的比较结论。

Method: 采用臂特定协变量调整（arm-specific covariate adjustment）：每个模型仅用自身运行产生（而非运行前测量）的训练日志统计量作为协变量进行调整，报告的效应仍然是原始均值差。在包含三种架构、三个数据集的视觉任务研究中，测试了基于早期训练日志的简单协变量调整方法，并与广泛搜索日志池中相关性最强统计量的大规模选择策略进行对比。

Result: 基于早期训练日志的简单调整在多数情况下能够降低模型比较的不确定性。然而，广泛搜索日志池以选取与结果最相关的统计量时，往往引入比消除更多的噪声，即使存在事后看来有用的统计量也是如此。

Conclusion: 训练日志在避免过大的选择噪声（selection noise）前提下，对于提高模型比较的精度是有效的工具；协变量选择是该方法的主要瓶颈，简单而非过度搜索的调整策略更为稳健。

Abstract: Comparing stochastically trained models requires estimating both a performance difference and its uncertainty from repeated runs. We study whether training logs from those same runs can make such comparisons more precise. Because training-log covariates are produced during training rather than measured before it, we use arm-specific covariate adjustment: each model is adjusted only with statistics from its own runs, and the raw mean difference remains the reported effect. In a vision study spanning three architectures and three datasets, simple adjustments based on early training logs often reduce uncertainty in model comparisons. The main limitation is covariate selection. Broadly searching the log pool for the most correlated statistic often adds more noise than it removes, even when useful statistics exist in hindsight. Training logs therefore appear useful for more precise model comparisons, but only when the adjustment avoids large selection noise.

</details>


### [141] [Designing a Good Virtual Node: Addressable and Cardinality-Preserving Global Memory for Message Passing Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02709)
*Félix Marcoccia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需自注意力、基于可寻址虚拟内存的消息传递神经网络增强方法，通过将全局内存分解为可独立写入/读取的状态（可寻址交叉注意力槽），并注入槽查询作为私有键/值锚点来保留基数信息，从而实现注入式多重集表示并支持1-WL细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的消息传递神经网络中的虚拟节点通过"节点→虚拟节点→节点"的管道将整图压缩成单一同质状态并广播给所有节点，造成有限容量瓶颈（homogeneous bottleneck）。作者希望不借助自注意力，而是通过辅助虚拟内存来缓解这一瓶颈。

Method: 基于Two-Radius分析识别两个关键要求：(1) 全局内存应分解为可独立写入和读取的状态，通过可寻址交叉注意力槽实现；(2) 仅可寻址性不足以保留多重性（multiset multiplicity），因为softmax注意力对均匀复制具有不变性。解决方法是将每个槽查询作为私有键/值锚点插入，恢复被丢弃的归一化质量，从而在有界颜色域上获得注入式多重集表示，能够实现1-WL细化。

Result: 在多重性感知的Two-Radius、子图（motif）计数和约束链接集预测等实验上验证了该可寻址且保留基数性质的虚拟内存方法的有效性，计算复杂度为O(nMd)。

Conclusion: 可寻址的、保留基数的虚拟内存能够在不依赖自注意力的前提下有效缓解虚拟节点的有限容量瓶颈，在保持计算效率的同时增强消息传递神经网络的表达能力。

Abstract: Virtual nodes give message-passing neural networks a simple global communication route, but the standard node--VN--node pipeline compresses the graph into one homogeneous state and broadcasts it identically to every node. Building on the Two-Radius analysis of Mishayev et al., we ask how auxiliary virtual memory can relieve this finite-capacity bottleneck without self-attention. We identify two requirements. First, the global memory should be factorized into independently writable and readable states: this can be achieved using addressable cross-attention slots. Second, addressability alone does not preserve multiplicity, because softmax attention is invariant to uniform replication. Inserting each slot query as a private key/value anchor recovers the discarded normalization mass and yields, on bounded color domains, an injective multiset representation able to implement a 1-WL refinement. Experiments on multiplicity-aware Two-Radius, motif counting, and constrained link-set prediction support this addressable and cardinality-preserving virtual memory at (O(nMd)) arithmetic cost.

</details>


### [142] [Neural Networks with Local Converging Inputs for Efficient Options Pricing Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02778)
*Harris Cobb,Wenbo Hao,Yingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为NNLCI（局部收敛输入神经网络）的新方法，用于提升多资产期权定价数值方法的效率。NNLCI通过神经网络对粗网格和细网格的数值解进行局部校正，仅需少量高保真训练数据即可将细网格解的均方根误差（RMSE）降低约4-12倍。该方法在Black-Scholes方程下的一至三维现金或零期权以及Heston随机波动率模型下的单资产下入障碍看涨期权上得到验证，显示出低训练成本和强泛化能力，可显著降低高维实时期权交易和风险管理中的计算需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维多资产期权定价的数值方法通常计算成本高昂，难以满足实时交易和风险管理的需求。现有的粗网格方法精度不足，而细网格方法虽然精度更高但计算代价大。因此，需要一种既能保持高精度又具有高计算效率的方法来加速期权定价。

Method: 提出NNLCI（Neural Networks with Local Converging Inputs）框架。该方法建立一个神经网络，以粗网格解和细网格解的差值作为局部校正的输入，通过训练网络学习两者之间的误差模式，从而对细网格数值解进行局部修正以逼近精确解。采用最简洁的输入格式以提高效率，并仅在少量参数组合的子集上进行训练，实现低训练成本。

Result: 在一至三维Black-Scholes方程下的现金或零期权以及Heston模型下的单资产下入障碍看涨期权（二维PDE）上进行了验证。在所有情形下，NNLCI将细网格数值解的RMSE降低了约4-12倍，即使仅在小部分参数组合上训练，也能在测试集上保持良好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: NNLCI能够以极低的训练成本显著提升数值期权定价方法的精度，具有较强的泛化能力，可有效降低高维问题在实时交易和风险管理中的计算需求，为多资产期权定价提供了一种高效且实用的加速方案。

Abstract: We present a novel application of Neural Networks with Local Converging Inputs (NNLCI) to improve the efficiency of existing numerical methods for pricing multi-asset options. The most concise input format for NNLCI has been introduced, offering substantial convenience and efficiency. NNLCI uses a neural network to locally correct solutions from a coarse mesh and a refined mesh (relative to the coarse one), requiring only a minimal amount of high-fidelity training data. We demonstrate this approach on cash-or-nothing options under the Black-Scholes equation in one, two, and three spatial dimensions, and on single-asset down-and-out barrier call options under the Heston stochastic-volatility model (whose pricing PDE is two-dimensional in the spot price $S$ and the instantaneous variance $v$). In each case, NNLCI reduces the root-mean-square error (RMSE) of the refined-mesh numerical solution by a factor of approximately 4-12 on test sets, even when the neural network is trained on only a small subset of parameter combinations. These results demonstrate that NNLCI significantly reduces computational requirements for high-dimensional problems in real-time options trading and risk management, offering low training costs and strong generalization ability.

</details>


### [143] [Evaluation Blindness: How Silent Measurement Failures Corrupt AI Systems from Training to Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02786)
*Priyanka Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"评估失明"(evaluation blindness)概念，指测量函数在系统实际失败时仍产生与健康状态无法区分的读数。该问题统一了训练期和部署期两个生命周期阶段，并通过正式的可检测性谓词、四项训练期案例研究、六类生产故障分类法（基于50起真实事件验证，其中53%为静默失败）以及失败预算框架加以论证，结论是测量基础设施是整个AI生命周期的正确性关切。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统可能静默失败，失败会穿过训练循环、评估管道和生产监控栈，直到下游危害显现才被察觉。现有文献将训练期和部署期的失败分别处理，缺乏统一的正式框架来描述和检测这类"看不见的失败"。

Method: 1) 形式化定义评估失明(evaluation blindness)和可检测性谓词，统一训练期与部署期两个阶段；2) 四项训练期案例研究，包括TRL PR #6594中的真实实现bug（损失正常下降但梯度被破坏）；3) 从法庭文件和监管文件验证的50起真实事故中构建六类故障分类法；4) 提出失败预算框架，将可接受的失败率与用例风险等级关联。

Result: 六类故障分类法经50起真实事故验证，发现53%可验证的公开失败是静默的。其中Operational类别的失败按结构定义100%静默。论文还展示了训练期案例中的具体机制失效，包括奖励模型被博弈、重要性采样校正被静默误算、基准污染膨胀微调评估，以及真实bug中梯度损坏伴随正常损失下降。

Conclusion: 测量基础设施是贯穿整个AI生命周期的正确性关切，而不仅仅是评估阶段的问题。评估失明现象需要系统性、跨阶段的形式化检测方法，失败预算框架可为不同风险等级的应用设定可接受的失败率阈值。

Abstract: AI systems can fail silently. The failure propagates through training loops, evaluation pipelines, and production monitoring stacks until downstream harm makes it visible. This paper introduces evaluation blindness: a measurement function M exhibits evaluation blindness with respect to failure class F when it produces readings indistinguishable from a healthy state while the system is actually failing, with no auxiliary signal flagging the gap.
  The problem surfaces at two lifecycle stages the literature has treated separately. At training time, reward models are gamed, importance-sampling corrections are silently miscalculated, and benchmark contamination inflates fine-tuning evaluations, all while loss curves look healthy and gradient updates proceed normally. At deployment time, monitoring fails to catch six classes of production failure, including an Operational category that is 100% silent by structural definition.
  We provide a formal detectability predicate unifying both stages. Four training-time case studies trace concrete breakdowns, including a real implementation bug in TRL PR #6594 where gradients are corrupted as loss decreases normally. A six-class taxonomy validated against 50 real-world incidents from court documents and regulatory filings finds that 53% of verifiable public failures were silent. A failure budget framework ties acceptable failure rates to use-case risk class.
  The implication is direct: measurement infrastructure is a correctness concern across the full AI lifecycle, not just at evaluation time. Data, code, and taxonomy schema are at https://github.com/priyanka25aug/llm-failure-taxonomy.

</details>


### [144] [Topological Simplification in Predictive Coding Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02816)
*Adam Shaw,Jiayu Li,Michael Sperling,Michael Kim,Alvin Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用分层持久同调分析研究预测编码网络（PCNs）学习表示的拓扑结构，发现较小的PCN更早跨层折叠连通分量，简化发生的深度与重构误差呈负相关，且PCN比匹配的MLP平均晚3.6层折叠连通分量，表明持久同调为PCN中的压缩-重构权衡提供了量化视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码网络是一种受神经科学启发的双向架构，但其学习表示的拓扑特性尚不清楚。作者希望通过持久同调这一拓扑数据分析工具，定量理解PCN中压缩与重构之间的权衡是如何随层、模型容量和架构变化的。

Method: 作者在合成分类数据集（测试准确率≥99.9%）和MNIST（≥95%）上训练表现良好的PCN，采用逐层持久同调分析量化不同架构和激活函数下拓扑特征随层的变化。通过Spearman相关系数分析模型大小与连通分量折叠的关系，用bootstrap方法进行跨架构和激活函数的种子级比较，并分析简化深度与重构误差的相关性。

Result: 主要发现包括：（1）较小的PCN比较大型模型更早跨层折叠连通分量（Spearman ρ∈[0.72,0.79]）；（2）简化深度与重构误差之间存在强负相关（ρ=-0.58），即简化发生更晚的架构重构效果更好；（3）PCN比匹配的MLP平均晚3.6层折叠连通分量。

Conclusion: 持久同调为PCN中的压缩-重构权衡提供了有用的定量分析工具。结果表明模型容量和预测编码推理中固有的循环双向动力学共同决定了该权衡在层间何时被解决，揭示了PCN区别于前馈MLP的独特拓扑特性。

Abstract: We study the topology of learned representations in predictive coding networks (PCNs), a neuro-inspired bidirectional architecture, using a quantitative layer-wise persistent homology analysis. We train well-performing PCNs on a synthetic classification dataset ($\geq 99.9\%$ test accuracy) and on MNIST ($\geq 95\%$ test accuracy), and measure how topological features change across layers for different architectures and activation functions. We find that smaller PCNs collapse connected components across layers earlier than larger models (Spearman $\unicode{x1D70C} \in [0.72, 0.79]$ across activations), with model size measured as the sum of hidden-layer widths. We also observe a strong negative correlation ($\unicode{x1D70C} = -0.58$) between the depth at which simplification occurs and reconstruction error; i.e., architectures that simplify later reconstruct better. Finally, a seed-level bootstrap comparison across architectures and activations shows that PCNs consistently collapse connected components later than matched MLPs, with an average difference of $3.6$ layers. These results suggest that persistent homology offers a useful quantitative lens on the compression--reconstruction tradeoff in PCNs, and that both model capacity and the recurrent, bidirectional dynamics of predictive coding inference shape when this tradeoff is resolved across layers.

</details>


### [145] [Wiring Beats Blending: What Transfers Between Transformer Sizes -- and What Doesn't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02829)
*Ravi Satya Durga Prasad Yenugula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了预训练大模型能否转换为更小规模的"兄弟"模型（1.4B→410M的Pythia家族转换）。研究发现表示层在大小模型间高度对齐（R²=0.84），但参数层对齐弱；直接权重投影在功能上具有破坏性。转换的价值在于初始化：通过最小二乘补偿（最佳零样本功能）和方差保持重缩放（最佳动态终点）两个独立杠杆，在匹配预算持续预训练中，补偿在低预算下显著优于子克隆变体（30M token时3/3种子胜出），但在33倍更大预算下收敛至持平。该方法的边界在~5倍供体规模（6.9B→1.4B）时出现，双杠杆叠加过度校正源于大宽度下补偿求解的病态条件，需维度感知正则化修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型家族通常从零开始训练每个规模。作者提出一个核心问题：预训练的大模型能否被转换为更小的"兄弟"模型？这一探索旨在理解跨规模知识转移的可能性，以及转换过程中哪些因素真正决定价值。

Method: 针对Pythia家族1.4B→410M的转换进行端到端表征：(i)量化表示对齐（ridge R²=0.84）与参数对齐的差异；(ii)证明稠密权重投影的功能破坏性——通过基混合破坏Rotary、per-head、GELU和LayerNorm结构来排除组装伪影；(iii)用最佳线性算子后的权重残差与噪声进行洗牌对照统计检验；(iv)在匹配预算的持续预训练中，将转换分解为两个独立杠杆——最小二乘补偿（优化零样本功能）和方差保持重缩放（优化动态终态）。实验涵盖不同宽度缩小对、深度缩小对、33倍更大预算、以及~5倍供体规模（6.9B→1.4B）的边界测试。

Result: 表示对齐强（R²=0.84）而参数对齐弱；稠密权重投影功能上具有破坏性且非组装伪影；最佳线性算子后残差与噪声统计不可区分，说明转换价值存储在初始化中。最小二乘补偿在30M token低预算下以84.0±1.8 vs 89.7±3.7（宽度缩小对）和109.3 vs 117.9（深度缩小对）超过最强子克隆变体，3/3种子胜出且更省token；33倍预算下两者收敛至持平（40.0 vs 40.0），均远超从零训练（低预算下最多18倍优势）。在6.9B→1.4B时双杠杆过度校正，根因是大宽度下补偿求解病态条件。

Conclusion: 跨规模转换的价值本质上存在于初始化，而非参数层面的直接投影。最小二乘补偿和方差保持重缩放是两个正交的有效杠杆，前者在低预算下是token高效的最优选择，后者优化动态收敛。该方法具有规模边界，需通过维度感知正则化解决大宽度下的病态条件问题。研究提供了完整代码、检查点和冻结评测语料库。

Abstract: Model families train every size from scratch. Can a pretrained large model be converted into a smaller sibling? We characterize the 1.4B->410M conversion in the Pythia family end-to-end: (i) representations align strongly across sizes (ridge R^2=0.84) while parameters align weakly; (ii) dense weight projection is functionally destructive -- provably not an assembly artifact -- because basis mixing breaks rotary, per-head, GELU, and LayerNorm structure; (iii) after the best-fit linear operator, weight residuals are statistically indistinguishable from noise under shuffle controls; (iv) conversion value therefore lives in initialization. In matched-budget continued pre-training we decompose conversion into two independent levers -- least-squares compensation (function: best zero-shot) and variance-preserving rescale (dynamics: best endpoints). Compensation is a token-efficient, low-budget win rather than a universal one: at 30M tokens it beats the strongest subcloning variant on both a width-reduced pair (84.0 +/- 1.8 vs. 89.7 +/- 3.7, 3/3 seeds) and a held-out depth-reduced pair (109.3 vs. 117.9, 3/3 seeds), reaching a given quality with fewer tokens; at a 33x larger budget the two converge to parity (40.0 vs. 40.0), both far ahead of from-scratch, which transfer initialization always beats -- by up to 18x at low budget, the margin narrowing at convergence and at the largest scale. We further map the method's boundary: at ~5x the donor scale (6.9B->1.4B) stacking both levers over-corrects, which we trace to ill-conditioning of the compensation solve at large width, pointing to dimension-aware regularization as the fix. Code, checkpoints, and the frozen evaluation corpus are released.

</details>


### [146] [NOMADD: Numerical Optimization of Models Adapting to Data Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02845)
*Swapn Shah,Keith Burghardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种后处理式的概念漂移缓解方法，适用于从树模型到神经网络再到表格基础模型的各种模型。算法将基础模型分别拟合到每个标注训练周期，度量其参数相对锚定模型的变化，通过低秩分解压缩这些变化，并用阻尼正则化预测外推每个潜在因子。在18个数据集的Drift-Resilient TabPFN基准上，该方法对每种基础模型族都有提升，且训练仅需数秒，远优于需要约1300 GPU小时预训练的Drift-Resilient TabPFN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格模型在时序数据上会因为数据漂移和概念漂移而性能退化。现有的漂移缓解工具通常针对神经网络架构定制，且需要改变训练方式，缺乏适用于多种模型的通用后处理方法。

Method: 1) 将基础模型分别拟合到每个标注训练时间周期；2) 度量模型参数相对于全部周期汇总池化得到的锚定模型的变化；3) 用低秩分解压缩参数变化；4) 用阻尼正则化预测（damped regularized forecast）对每个潜在因子进行外推。

Result: 在Drift-Resilient TabPFN基准的18个数据集上，在基准自身协议和指标下，该方法对所有基础模型族都有性能提升，性能匹敌SOTA的Drift-Resilient TabPFN，但训练仅需数秒；而Drift-Resilient TabPFN需要在约1300个GPU小时上对数百万合成数据集进行预训练，且推理速度慢多个数量级。

Conclusion: 低秩因子外推是缓解概念漂移的有效且高效的通用后处理工具，对表格模型广泛适用，并展现出扩展到其他模态的潜力。

Abstract: Tabular model performance degrades when feature distributions change over time or the relationship between features and outcome variables change over time, known as data drift and concept drift, respectively. These issues are challenging to mitigate in real time because labeled data may not be immediately available, or re-training a model could be impractical. While tools exist to reduce drift, they are typically bespoke to neural network architectures and adapt how models are trained. In this paper, we offer an alternative post-hoc method to reduce concept drift, which is applicable to a variety of models, from trees to neural networks to tabular foundation models. This new tool is especially useful when constraints, such as high model accuracy, bounded inference time, or model size requires users to choose between different models for their specific use-cases. Our algorithm fits the base model separately on each labeled training period, measures how its parameters evolve against a single anchor model pooled over all of those periods, compresses those changes with a low-rank factorization, and extrapolates each latent factor forward with a damped, regularized forecast. On the 18-dataset Drift-Resilient TabPFN benchmark, evaluated under that benchmark's own protocol and metric, the extrapolation improves every base family it is applied to, and achieves performance competitive with the state-of-the-art Drift-Resilient TabPFN with seconds of training. In contrast, Drift-Resilient TabPFN requires pre-training on millions of synthetic datasets over approximately 1,300 GPU-hours, and is orders of magnitude slower in inference (depending on the model). In the discussion, we explore the promise and challenges of extending this tool to other modalities.

</details>


### [147] [Adaptive Sampling for Automated Post-Disaster Rapid Damage Assessment via Level-Set Cost-Aware Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02868)
*Boyang Xu,Mostafa Reisi Gahrooei,Mohammad Ilbeigi,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种成本感知的贝叶斯优化与水平集估计相结合的框架，引导无人机等自主数据采集器动态评估灾后建筑损伤，在降低不确定性的同时最小化运营成本，并通过合成数据和R2D高保真数据验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统灾后损伤评估依赖静态、劳动密集的数据采集方法，成本高昂且难以适应灾后动态变化的环境，亟需一种快速、可靠且经济高效的评估手段来支持应急响应。

Method: 将成本感知的贝叶斯优化与水平集估计相结合，持续引导自主数据采集器（如无人机）前往信息量最大的区域，通过动态更新不同地理区域的损伤估计，在最小化运营成本的同时系统性地降低预测不确定性。

Result: 在受控合成玩具研究中，代理能够高效追踪损伤边界、恢复底层损伤地图并快速降低预测不确定性；在R2D软件生成的高保真灾害数据上，算法提供了准确及时的损伤估计，支持信息充分且快速的应急响应。

Conclusion: 该框架能够实现成本高效的自主灾后损伤评估，兼顾准确性与时效性，为应急响应决策提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Natural disasters frequently inflict severe damage to the built environment, which demands a rapid, reliable, and cost-effective damage assessment for emergency response. However, traditional methods for post-disaster damage assessment often rely on static, labor-intensive data collection strategies that can be prohibitively expensive and struggle to adapt to dynamic post-disaster conditions. In this study, we propose a cost-aware Bayesian optimization framework combined with level-set estimation that continuously guides autonomous data collectors, e.g., an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV), toward the most informative regions. By dynamically updating damage estimates across different geographic zones, our approach systematically reduces uncertainty while minimizing operational costs. The proposed framework is first validated using a controlled synthetic toy study, demonstrating the agent's ability to efficiently trace damage boundaries, recover the underlying damage map, and rapidly reduce predictive uncertainty. Furthermore, the approach is evaluated using high-fidelity disaster data generated by the Regional Resilience Determination (R2D) software. The results of the algorithm provide accurate and timely damage estimates that support informative and fast emergency response.

</details>


### [148] [Contrast-invariant deep ptychography neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02869)
*Albert Vong,Steven Henke,Oliver Hoidn,Hanna Ruth,Junjing Deng,Apurva Mehta,David Shapiro,Alexander Hexemer,Nicholas Schwarz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对叠层衍射成像神经网络（Ptychography）在分布外泛化时存在的尺度不一致问题，提出了一种因子分解策略，将学习的物体纹理与测量尺度解耦，使得单个训练好的网络能够在不同光照条件下产生与测量一致的图像重建结果。同时引入合成对象采样策略以减少合成训练数据与实验目标之间的相位分布失配，在5个跨束线及设施的实验数据集上将傅里叶误差较之前的PtychoPINN-torch基线降低了最多5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叠层衍射神经网络在分布外泛化时面临尺度不一致（scaling inconsistency）的问题，这严重限制了其在实际应用中的可行性。现有方法在处理不同光照条件等测量变化时，无法保持重建结果的测量一致性。

Method: 1. 提出因子分解（factorization）策略，将学习的物体纹理与测量尺度解耦，使单一训练网络能在不同光照条件下产生测量一致的重建结果。2. 将学到的物体以实部与虚部（real and imaginary units）表示而非传统的振幅与相位（amplitude and phase）表示。3. 引入合成对象采样策略，最小化合成训练数据与实验目标之间的相位分布失配。

Result: 在跨越多个束线和设施的5个实验数据集上，与之前的PtychoPINN-torch基线相比，傅里叶误差最多降低了5倍。

Conclusion: 通过解耦物体纹理与测量尺度、采用实虚部表示以及改进合成数据采样策略，有效解决了叠层衍射神经网络分布外泛化时的尺度不一致问题，显著提升了模型在实际实验数据上的重建精度。

Abstract: Ptychography neural networks suffer from scaling inconsistencies when generalizing out of distribution, limiting their real world viability. We address this scaling mismatch using a factorization strategy which decouples the learned object texture from measurement scaling, enabling a single trained network to produce measurement-consistent reconstructions across varying illumination conditions. This requires predicting the learned object in real and imaginary units instead of the canonical amplitude and phase representation. We additionally introduce a synthetic object sampling strategy that minimizes phase distribution mismatch between synthetic training data and experimental targets. These improvements yield up to a 5x reduction in Fourier error over the previous PtychoPINN-torch baseline across 5 experimental datasets spanning multiple beamlines and facilities.

</details>


### [149] [Maglev: Sliding Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02870)
*Bo Liu,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带固定大小记忆的循环Transformer架构，通过预填充器Q与解码器P的双模型耦合训练，使用记忆一致性损失对齐两者记忆，使推理时仅用P即可获得超越滑动窗口注意力和潜在循环Transformer的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑动窗口注意力虽然高效但视野受限，而全注意力虽表达力强但计算开销大。作者希望设计一种既能保持训练并行化、又能在推理时具备高效固定大小记忆的Transformer架构，在效率与表达力之间取得更好平衡。

Method: 架构由两个耦合模型组成：(1) 预填充器Q，利用全注意力（实践中为全注意力与滑动窗口注意力交错）生成记忆目标m'_t；(2) 解码器P，仅使用滑动窗口注意力与循环K/V注入生成解码器记忆m_t用于下一token预测。训练时通过记忆一致性损失将m_t与m'_t对齐，推理时仅使用P。还探索了P与Q的参数共享以降低参数开销。

Result: 实验表明，该架构在验证损失和下游预训练基准上均优于滑动窗口注意力基线和潜在循环Transformer基线；参数共享在减少参数记忆的同时保留了大部分性能收益。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了通过教师-学生式的记忆蒸馏（Q引导P）能够使一个仅使用滑动窗口注意力的循环解码器获得接近全注意力的表达能力，为高效长上下文Transformer提供了一种可行的设计范式。

Abstract: We introduce \ours{}, a recurrent Transformer architecture with fixed-size memory that generalizes sliding-window attention while remaining parallelizable during training. \ours{} consists of two coupled models: a prefiller $Q$, which leverages full attention\footnote{In practice, we use interleaved full and sliding-window attention for $Q$, as this yields stronger performance. The essential requirement is that $Q$ be more expressive than $P$, with access to the full history.} to produce memory targets $m'_t$, and a decoder $P$, which uses only sliding-window attention and recurrent K/V injection to produce decoder memories $m_t$ for next-token prediction. We train \ours{} with a memory consistency loss that aligns $m_t$ with $m'_t$, allowing inference to use $P$ alone. Empirically, \ours{} improves validation loss and downstream pretraining benchmarks over sliding-window and latent recurrent transformer baselines. Moreover, sharing parameters between $P$ and $Q$ reduces parameter memory while preserving most of the gains.

</details>


### [150] [GoT-CD: Graph-of-Thoughts Causal Discovery and the Fragility of Post-hoc Path-Specific Fairness Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02877)
*Nitish Nagesh,Elahe Khatibi,Thomas Dean Hughes,Mahdi Bagheri,Pratik Gajane,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GoT-CD，一种基于Graph-of-Thoughts推理的因果发现方法，将完整候选边集作为推理单元，通过并行生成、确定性有效性评分、硬并集约束和贪心投影来保证DAG结构。结果显示GoT-CD在五个基准上均返回有效DAG，在Asia、Alzheimer's和COVID-Respiratory数据集上取得最佳DAG有效F1分数，同时揭示结构保真度并不保证路径特定公平性审计的忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径特定反事实公平性估计依赖预先给定的因果图，因此继承因果发现步骤引入的错误。现有发现方法仅按聚合结构指标评估，没有评估特定路径是否能被恢复，以及路径缺失时审计会报告什么。需要一种能够保证DAG有效性且评估公平性忠实性的发现方法。

Method: GoT-CD采用全图Graph-of-Thoughts推理，推理单元是完整候选边集。多个候选图并行生成，通过确定性有效性函数评分，在硬并集约束（禁止虚构边）下合并，并通过贪心投影确保最终结果为DAG。在评估中结合路径特定审计，检查敏感属性到结果的非法路径是否被恢复。

Result: GoT-CD在全部五个基准数据集上均返回有效DAG，在Asia、Alzheimer's和COVID-Respiratory上取得LLM方法中最佳DAG有效F1分数。但在Alzheimer's基准上，八个发现图中五个未恢复敏感属性到结果的路径，导致路径特定审计报告零总效应而中介效应仍存在。

Conclusion: 结构保真度不保证公平性忠实度。仅靠好的图结构指标不足以支撑可靠的路径特定公平性审计，需要将路径特定公平性分析纳入结构发现流程，作为因果发现下游评估的必要组成部分。

Abstract: Causal discovery recovers directed structure from observational data and is increasingly used in clinical settings to support mechanism reasoning and fairness audits of predictive models. Path-specific counterfactual fairness asks whether a protected attribute influences an outcome through illegitimate pathways, but these estimands are defined relative to a supplied causal graph and therefore inherit whatever errors the discovery step introduces. Discovery methods are routinely scored on aggregate structural metrics that weight all edges equally, and no established evaluation asks whether the specific pathway an audit depends on survives discovery---or what the audit reports when that pathway is missing. Here we show that full-graph Graph-of-Thoughts reasoning yields acyclic discovered graphs that are structurally competitive with large language model (LLM) baselines, yet that structural fidelity alone does not guarantee fairness-faithful audits. We introduce GoT-CD, in which the reasoning unit is a complete candidate edge set: multiple graphs are generated in parallel, scored by a deterministic validity function, and merged under a hard union constraint that forbids invented edges, with greedy projection enforcing a DAG before commitment. GoT-CD returns a valid DAG on all five reported benchmarks and achieves the best DAG-valid F1 score among LLM methods on Asia, Alzheimer's, and COVID-Respiratory datasets. On an Alzheimer's benchmark with known unfair path, a post-hoc path-specific audit shows that five of eight discovered graphs recover no path from the sensitive attribute to the outcome and therefore report a null overall effect while mediated effects persist, necessitating downstream path-specific fairness analysis along with structural discovery.

</details>


### [151] [Population-Robust Feature Selection via Generalized Welfare Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02887)
*Ruiqi Lyu,Alistair Turcan,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PopFS方法，用于学习一个共享且可部署的特征集，该特征集对人口差异具有鲁棒性，同时允许每个群体训练自己的模型。通过可调福利目标平衡整体预测收益与最弱势群体的保护，并利用多任务稀疏学习缩减候选池、对硬特征集进行直接搜索，在多个数据集和人口划分上取得了优异的平均及最差群体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际部署中，同一套有限的问卷、测试面板或传感器组需要服务于多个异质性群体，但现有特征选择方法通常只针对单一大规模群体进行优化，而现有的鲁棒方法往往为所有群体学习一个共享模型。这两类方法都无法很好地兼顾"共享特征集"与"各群体独立建模"的需求，因此需要一个既能部署单一特征集、又能对不同人群差异保持鲁棒性的方法。

Method: PopFS核心方法包括：（1）采用可调福利目标（welfare objective），使从业者可以在整体预测收益与对受益最少群体的更强保护之间进行权衡；（2）为在规模上可行，先使用多任务稀疏学习（multitask sparse learning）缩减候选特征池；（3）随后直接对硬特征集进行搜索，通过对有前景的候选特征进行排名、添加和交换操作，并且只对候选短名单进行完全的模型重拟合。

Result: 在来自五个表格和公共卫生数据集的六个预测任务、八个人口划分上，PopFS在平均性能和最差群体性能上都持续表现优异，同时可扩展到数千个候选特征。在43个州的COVID-19即时预测研究中，调整福利目标能够在几乎不影响平均性能的情况下改善最弱势州的性能，并带来可解释的所选症状信号变化。

Conclusion: PopFS为异构人群中共享特征集的部署提供了一种实用的解决方案，能够在兼顾整体预测效益的同时保护受益最少的群体，且具有较好的可扩展性和可解释性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Choosing which features to collect is a deployment decision: the same limited questionnaire, test panel, or sensor set may need to serve several heterogeneous populations. Standard feature-selection methods typically optimize for one large population, while existing robust approaches tend to learn one shared model for every population. We introduce PopFS, a method for learning one shared, deployable feature set that is robust to population differences while letting each pop- ulation train its own model. PopFS uses a tunable welfare objective that lets practitioners balance overall predictive ben- efit against stronger protection of the populations that benefit least. To make this objective practical at scale, PopFS first uses multitask sparse learning to reduce the candidate pool, then searches directly over hard feature sets by ranking promising additions and swaps and fully refitting only a shortlist. Across eight population splits from six prediction tasks drawn from five tabular and public-health datasets, PopFS consistently achieves strong average and worst-population performance while scaling to thousands of candidate features. A 43-state COVID-19 nowcasting study further shows that changing the welfare objective can improve the least-served states with lit- tle change in average performance and yields an interpretable change in the selected symptom signals. Our code is available at https://github.com/Rachel-Lyu/PopFS.

</details>


### [152] [Robust Counterfactual Policy Optimisation via Nondeterministic Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02893)
*Jessica Lally,Milad Kazemi,Nicola Paoletti,David Watson,Sander Beckers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在概率非确定性因果模型下形式化反事实策略优化，将潜在混淆与不可约随机性分离，提出敏感性分析框架下的鲁棒反事实策略识别问题，并在败血症治疗模拟器上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反事实推理方法通常假设确定性因果模型（随机性全部来源于潜在变量），但MDP本质上具有随机性，这导致现有方法无法正确处理真实世界中的随机决策问题。

Method: 形式化概率非确定性因果模型下的反事实策略优化，将潜在混淆与不可约随机性正确分离，并在敏感性分析框架下提出首个实用的鲁棒反事实策略优化问题。

Result: 在败血症治疗模拟器上验证了方法有效性，该模拟器中糖尿病状态作为隐藏的全局混淆变量。

Conclusion: 通过概率非确定性因果模型和敏感性分析框架，能够在存在随机性与潜在混淆的场景下识别鲁棒的反事实策略，弥补了现有方法的不足。

Abstract: Counterfactual inference approaches for sequential decision-making typically assume deterministic causal models, where all randomness stems from latent variables. However, Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) are inherently stochastic. We address this by formalising counterfactual policy optimisation under probabilistic nondeterministic causal models, which properly separates latent confounding from irreducible stochasticity, and here propose a first practical optimisation problem for identifying robust counterfactual policies under a sensitivity analysis framework. We validate our approach on a sepsis treatment simulator, where diabetes status acts as a hidden global confounder.

</details>


### [153] [AnchorKV: Anchor-Residual KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02901)
*Malik Khalaf,Yara Shamshoum,Nitzan Hodos,Yuval Sieradzki,Assaf Schuster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AnchorKV是一种KV缓存压缩方案，通过锚点精确存储+相似性近似表示，在不丢弃任何token的情况下实现20倍压缩，在70B规模下保留全缓存99%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理中KV缓存是主要内存瓶颈。现有方法存在两难：驱逐方法永久丢弃token导致性能下降（被丢弃token可能后来被证明关键）；量化方法虽保留所有token但精度低，压缩有限。

Method: AnchorKV将KV缓存表示为一小组精确存储的锚点（anchors），每个非锚点token通过其最相似的锚点来表达；仅对近似误差最影响模型输出的token进行精化处理。

Result: AnchorKV在多种模型和数据集上一致地保持精度，在70B规模下保留了全缓存分数的99%，同时将整个上下文的存储成本降低到原先的一小部分（20倍压缩）。

Conclusion: AnchorKV通过锚点机制在不丢弃任何token的前提下实现了比现有方法更优的压缩率与精度保留，有效解决了驱逐与量化两条技术路线的固有缺陷。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache is the primary memory bottleneck in long-context LLM inference. Existing approaches attack it from opposite ends: eviction methods permanently discard tokens, degrading performance whenever a discarded token later proves essential, while quantization methods retain all tokens at low precision but offer limited compression. We propose AnchorKV, a compression scheme that shrinks the cache by $20\times$ without discarding a single token. AnchorKV represents the cache using a small set of anchors stored exactly, expresses every other token through its most similar anchor, and refines only those whose approximation most affects the model's output. AnchorKV consistently preserves accuracy across models and datasets, retaining 99% of the full-cache score at the 70B scale, while keeping the entire context at a fraction of its cost.

</details>


### [154] [Bayesian Data Reweighting Improves Multimodal Retrieval for Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02907)
*Jingchen Sun,Shaobo Han,Ruiyi Zhang,Naresh Kumar Devulapally,Ming Liu,Yitao Long,Vishnu Suresh Lokhande,Changyou Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯数据重加权（Bayesian Data Reweighting）框架，将查询-文档重要性建模为潜变量，通过共轭先验下的闭式后验更新和随机EM优化，自适应降低假阴性样本的权重，从而提升多模态检索器在知识型视觉问答中的检索准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态检索器对比训练方法将所有不匹配的查询-文档对视为同等信息量的负样本，但许多不匹配文档实际上在语义上相关或部分有用，作为假阴性会损害检索器的训练效果。

Method: 提出贝叶斯数据重加权框架，将查询-文档重要性建模为潜变量，利用共轭先验获得闭式后验更新，并通过随机EM优化自适应推断后验权重，以降低假阴性样本的权重。

Result: 在三种检索器和七个基于知识型VQA基准上一致提升了检索准确率。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯数据重加权能够有效缓解对比学习中假阴性样本问题，显著提升多模态检索器在知识型VQA任务上的表现。

Abstract: Multimodal retrievers are essential for knowledge-based visual question answering, where they retrieve external evidence for image-question pairs. However, existing contrastive training methods typically treat all unmatched query-document pairs as equally informative negatives, which is problematic because many unmatched documents may still be semantically relevant or partially useful. We propose Bayesian Data Reweighting, a probabilistic framework that models query-document importance as latent variables and adaptively infers posterior weights to downweight likely false negatives. With closed-form posterior updates under conjugate priors and stochastic EM optimization, our method consistently improves retrieval accuracy across three retrievers and seven knowledge-based VQA benchmarks.

</details>


### [155] [Forecasting Revenue with its Customer-Base Drivers: When and Why Coordination Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02911)
*Kyeongbin Kim,Daniel McCarthy,Dokyun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出客户基础多任务Transformer（CBMT）模型，利用966家公司的周交易面板数据，通过共享表示学习与原始分预测对齐，实现收入预测的协调与改进。CBMT的总销售额平均误差比最强基准低30%，在74.3%的公司中优于单任务预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合收入预测无法揭示收入变化的构成（新客户获取、重复购买、客单价、对冲变动），而现有方法无法同时提供准确的总收入预测和可解释的"原始"（primitive）客户基础预测，且二者之间的协调关系未被充分利用。

Method: 作者利用966家公司、25个行业、每周交易面板数据，开发了客户基础多任务Transformer（CBMT）。该模型通过多任务学习共享结构，同时保留对每个原始成分（获取、复购、支出等）的独立预测，并将这些预测的组合与下游总收入对齐进行联合优化。此外，文中还比较了校准期路由规则与始终部署CBMT的差异。

Result: CBMT的总销售额平均误差比最强代表性客户基础基准低30%，比直接预测总收入的Transformer低2.65%（配对差异未达统计显著，p=.222），在74.3%的公司中优于单独估计的单任务预测。在24个基准-结果组合比较中，CBMT的源MAE在23个中更低，剩余1个差异不显著。原始成分协动越强的公司越可能受益于联合预测。当客户基础动态高度波动时，所有模型精度下降且CBMT优势收窄。校准期路由规则不优于始终部署CBMT。

Conclusion: CBMT展示了协调的客户基础预测能够支持收入规划，但准确性在客户基础动态高度波动时下降且优势收窄，提示模型在稳定环境下更具价值。选定的族场景-3比较结果与共享表示和收入对齐的收益一致，但属于诊断性而非因果性证据。

Abstract: Revenue forecasts guide acquisition budgets, demand planning, and customer-based valuations, yet an aggregate forecast does not show whether change reflects acquisition, repeat purchasing, spending per order, or offsetting movements. Using weekly transaction panels for 966 companies in 25 industries, the authors develop the Customer-Based Multi-task Transformer (CBMT), which learns shared structure, retains separate primitive forecasts, and aligns their combination with downstream revenue. CBMT's mean total-sales error is 30% below the strongest representative established customer-base benchmark. It is also 2.65% below a Transformer that forecasts total sales directly, although the paired difference is not statistically significant (p=.222), and it beats separately estimated single-task forecasts for 74.3% of firms. CBMT's source MAE is lower in 23 of 24 benchmark-by-outcome comparisons, with the remaining difference not statistically distinguishable from zero. Firms whose primitives co-move more strongly are more likely to benefit from joint forecasting; selected-family scenario-3 comparisons are consistent with gains from shared representation and revenue alignment but remain diagnostic rather than causal. Accuracy deteriorates for all models when customer-base dynamics are highly volatile, and CBMT's advantage narrows there. Calibration-period routing rules do not improve average accuracy over always deploying CBMT. The results show how coordinated customer-base forecasts support revenue planning and when they warrant greater caution.

</details>


### [156] [When Should Graph Attention Be Sparse? Learning a Per-Edge Tsallis Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02938)
*Kleyton da Costa,Bernardo Modenesi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LTGA提出可学习的Tsallis熵指数q来替代固定的softmax注意力归一化，在全局标量到逐边索引的四个粒度上连续插值重尾、softmax和紧支撑的注意力分布。实验发现LTGA-Edge平均排名最优（2.75），但omnibus检验未拒绝零假设（p=0.199），学习q并不优于网格搜索——验证调优的冻结网格达61.4%、α-entmax达62.2%、容量匹配的q≡1对照达62.0%，而LTGA-Edge为61.7%。学习q的价值在于单次运行即可替代网格搜索，且提供可解释机制：q偏离1时会将42%的注意力系数剪枝为精确零，且这些边是选择性的错误边，恢复它们损失7.1个点，而同样比例的随机剪枝损失13.0个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图注意力机制用softmax（Shannon统计下的最大熵选择）归一化邻域分数，但同配图和异配图需要不同的注意力形状，单一固定归一化无法同时满足两者需求。

Method: 提出LTGA（可学习Tsallis图注意力）层，其Tsallis熵指数q与网络权重联合学习，在从全局标量到逐边索引的四个粒度上连续插值重尾（q<1）、softmax（q=1）和紧支撑（q>1）注意力，采用有界重参数化使每个模型从GAT基线出发。

Result: 在8个基准数据集的10个种子下，LTGA-Edge取得最佳平均排名（2.75），但omnibus检验未达显著（p=0.199）。学习q未优于搜索q：验证调优的冻结网格61.4%、调优α-entmax 62.2%、容量匹配的q≡1对照62.0%，LTGA-Edge为61.7%。学习q的实际收益是单次运行替代网格搜索，且q偏离1时剪掉42%的注意力系数为精确零，剪枝边是选择性的错误边（恢复损失7.1点，随机剪枝同比例损失13.0点）。

Conclusion: LTGA证明可学习Tsallis熵指数能在单一前向中动态适配图注意力形状，虽在性能上不显著优于网格搜索基线，但提供了显著的效率优势（免去网格搜索）和可解释的剪枝机制，q偏离1时精确剪除的错误边具有较高标识价值。

Abstract: Graph attention normalizes neighborhood scores with softmax, the maximum-entropy choice under Shannon statistics. But homophilic and heterophilic graphs want different attention shapes, and one fixed normalization cannot serve both. We propose \textbf{LTGA} (\textbf{L}earnable \textbf{T}sallis \textbf{G}raph \textbf{A}ttention), a graph attention layer whose Tsallis entropic index $q$ is learned jointly with the weights, interpolating continuously between heavy-tailed ($q\!<\!1$), softmax ($q\!=\!1$) and compact-support ($q\!>\!1$) attention at four granularities from a global scalar to a per-edge index, under a bounded reparameterization that starts every model at the GAT baseline. Across eight benchmarks at ten seeds, LTGA-Edge takes the best average rank ($2.75$), but the omnibus test does not reject ($p\!=\!0.199$) and learning $q$ does not beat searching it: a validation-tuned frozen grid reaches $61.4\%$, tuned $α$-entmax $62.2\%$ and a capacity-matched $q\!\equiv\!1$ control $62.0\%$, against $61.7\%$ for LTGA-Edge. What the learned index buys is one run instead of a grid, and an interpretable mechanism: where $q$ leaves $1$, it prunes $42\%$ of attention coefficients to exactly zero, and those edges are selectively the wrong ones, restoring them costs $7.1$ points, while random pruning at the same rate costs $13.0$ more. Project page: https://kleyt0n.github.io/ltga

</details>


### [157] [Federated generative event models for tokenized electronic health records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02939)
*Michael C. Burkhart,Luke Solo,Inhyeok Lee,S'Khaja Charles,Zewei "Whiskey" Liao,Kaveri Chhikara,Dema Therese,Wan-Ting Liao,Catherine A. Gao,William F. Parker,Brett K. Beaulieu-Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了联邦训练的分词生成事件模型（GEM）在三个独立医疗系统、122,251次ICU住院数据上的表现。GEM在站点内和跨站点ROC-AUC上均优于传统监督模型，且跨站点性能损失更小（0.025 vs 0.079 ROC-AUC）。联邦学习（FedAvg/FedAvgM）接近集中式训练性能，大多数增益在5-10轮通信内获得。然而，集中式多站点训练对本地训练的优势有限，仅在本地数据稀缺时多站点模型才更有用。主要挑战在于学习可迁移的表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录基础模型受限于机构数据孤岛和跨站点迁移时的性能大幅下降。现有研究缺乏对联邦训练生成式模型在多卫生系统ICU数据上的系统评估，需要探索联邦学习能否在保护隐私的同时实现模型的可迁移性和中心化性能。

Method: 在三个独立卫生系统的122,251次ICU住院数据上（统一为Common Longitudinal ICU Data Format），训练分词生成事件模型（GEM），并在12个24小时后的临床预测任务上评估四种训练配置：站点内训练、跨站点迁移、集中式多站点训练和联邦学习（FedAvg与FedAvgM）。基准对比采用传统监督模型LightGBM。

Result: GEM在站点内和跨站点的平均ROC-AUC均为最高；跨站点性能损失远小于LightGBM（GEM为0.025 ROC-AUC和0.027 PR-AUC，LightGBM为0.079和0.089）。联邦学习接近集中式GEM训练性能，大多数增益在5-10轮内获得。但集中式多站点训练相对完全本地训练仅带来适度改进；多站点模型仅在本地训练数据有限时优势明显。

Conclusion: 联邦GEM训练在技术上可行且保留了集中式训练的大部分性能，但核心开放挑战在于学习可迁移的表征，以便将多个卫生系统更大但异构的数据转化为可靠的目标站点收益。

Abstract: Electronic health record foundation models are limited by institutionally siloed data and substantial performance degradation under cross-site transfer. We evaluated federated training of tokenized generative event models (GEMs) across 122,251 intensive care hospitalizations from three independent health systems harmonized to the Common Longitudinal ICU Data Format. Models were assessed on 12 post-24-hour clinical prediction tasks using within-site, cross-site, centralized, and federated training configurations. GEMs achieved the highest mean within-site and cross-site ROC-AUC and were substantially more transportable than conventional supervised models: their average cross-site penalties were 0.025 ROC-AUC and 0.027 PR-AUC, compared with 0.079 and 0.089 for LightGBM. Federated Learning (FedAvg and FedAvgM) approached the performance of centralized GEM training, with most gains obtained within 5-10 communication rounds. However, centralized multi-site training provided only modest improvements over complete local training. Multi-site models were most useful when local training data were limited, with their advantage narrowing as institutional data accumulated. These findings show that federated GEM training is technically feasible and preserves most centralized performance, but that the main open challenge is learning transportable representations to translate larger, but heterogeneous data from multiple health systems into a reliable target-site benefit.

</details>


### [158] [Sedentary Behavior Classification for Wearable Sensors with a CNN-BiLSTM Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02946)
*Yuliang Chen,Weiwei Shi,Jingjing Zou,Rong Zablocki,Animesh Kumar,Jordan A. Carlson,Sheri J. Hartman,Mikael Anne Greenwood-Hickman,Paul R. Hibbing,Marta Jankowska,Jay Yang,Arun Kumar,Loki Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将髋部佩戴的加速度计数据训练的深度学习模型（CHAP，CNN-BiLSTM架构）迁移到腕部佩戴数据进行坐姿与非坐姿分类，通过零样本测试和不同数量标注数据的微调进行评估，发现髋部预训练模型在腕部准确率下降但微调优于从头训练的Transformer模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 久坐行为与健康风险相关，准确检测久坐行为至关重要。然而，基于腕部佩戴传感器的姿态分类任务仍具挑战性，因此需要探索模型能否从髋部传感器数据迁移到腕部传感器数据，以缓解腕部数据标注成本高的问题。

Method: 使用CHAP（CNN-BiLSTM混合模型，最初为髋部加速度计设计）进行坐姿与非坐姿分类。研究中在iWatch数据集上评估两种迁移策略：(1) 零样本评估——髋部训练模型直接应用于腕部数据；(2) 微调——使用不同数量的腕部标注数据对模型进行领域适应。真实姿态标签来自佩戴式相机。

Result: 髋部训练模型在髋部数据上无需重训练即表现良好，但在腕部数据上因传感器位置偏移导致准确率下降。对CHAP进行微调较从头训练的Transformer模型具有一致的性能优势。

Conclusion: 髋部预训练可作为腕部部署的有效起点，但需针对腕部数据进行特定适应，以应对更高的信号变异性。

Abstract: Accurate detection of sedentary behavior is important for studying health risks related to prolonged sitting, but posture-based classification remains challenging with wearable sensors, especially at the wrist. We study whether a deep learning model trained on hip-worn accelerometer data can transfer to wrist-worn accelerometer data for sitting versus non-sitting classification. We use CHAP, a CNN-BiLSTM model originally developed for hip accelerometers, and evaluate its zero-shot performance on wrist data as well as its adaptation through finetuning with varying amounts of labeled wrist data. Experiments are conducted on the iWatch dataset with ground-truth posture labels derived from wearable cameras. The hip-trained model performs strongly on hip data without retraining, but accuracy drops on wrist data due to sensor placement shift. Finetuning CHAP provides consistent advantages over transformer models trained from scratch. These findings suggest that hip-based pretraining provides a useful starting point for wrist deployment, while highlighting the need for wrist-specific adaptation to handle higher signal variability.

</details>


### [159] [ATFlash: Per-RoPE-Wavelength Attention Windows for Compute/Memory-Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02947)
*Shun-ichiro Hayashi,Daichi Mukunoki,Tetsuya Hoshino,Takahiro Katagiri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于RoPE波长分解的注意力剪枝窗口方法。由于RoPE注意力分数可分解为各2D旋转频率对的贡献和，每对频率的波长限制了其位置区分能力。据此提出"按RoPE波长距离窗口"：剪除超过波长比例距离的query-key内积项。与滑窗不同，每个key仍可被访问（至少通过低频对）。剪枝率与输入无关，有闭合形式（对数于序列长度N）。该方法在token级选择之外，还可与MInference等频率级动态稀疏方法正交叠加。在Qwen2.5-0.5B和Llama-3.2-3B上，原生上下文长度内剪除37-48%的QK内积项，top-1匹配率保持96-98%，LongBench-v2上平均输出分布KL在10^-3 nat级别。长上下文基准（RULER、OpenAI-MRCR、LongCodeQA、∞Bench）分数大体保持。实现为QK收缩轴的一个切片，不触碰online-softmax递归，已以最小diff移植到FlashAttention-4 prefill和FlashInfer decode。在RTX PRO 6000+Llama上，两端口均超过stock版本，加速比随上下文增长，128K达1.29×。端到端在Qwen2.5-7B-1M上，剪除57%内积项，1M上下文加速达1.31×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE注意力分数按频率对分解的结构未被充分利用来减少注意力计算。现有动态稀疏方法（如MInference）输入依赖、无法保证剪枝率；滑窗虽简单但会完全切断远距离key的可达性。需要一种既保持全key可达、又具有输入无关剪枝率的静态剪枝方案。

Method: 利用RoPE注意力分数可精确分解为各2D旋转频率对的贡献之和这一数学性质。对每个RoPE波长，设置一个与波长成比例的剪枝距离窗口，剪除query-key内积中超出该距离的项。该窗口实现为query-key收缩轴上的一个切片操作，不改变online-softmax的递归结构。该方法在token级选择层面工作，与MInference等频率级动态稀疏方法正交，可叠加使用。已通过最小改动移植到FlashAttention-4 prefill和FlashInfer decode。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-0.5B和Llama-3.2-3B上剪除37-48%的QK内积项，top-1匹配率96-98%，LongBench-v2上KL散度在10^-3 nat级别。长上下文基准分数大致保持。在RTX PRO 6000上Llama加速比随上下文长度增长，128K达1.29×；Qwen2.5-7B-1M端到端1M上下文剪除57%内积项，加速1.31×。

Conclusion: RoPE的波长结构可被直接利用来实现高效、输入无关的注意力剪枝。该方法在保持注意力质量（top-1匹配率高、KL低、长上下文基准分数保持）的同时提供确定性的计算节省，且与现有动态稀疏方法正交，易于集成到主流attention kernel中并在超长上下文下获得显著加速。

Abstract: The attention score with rotary position embeddings (RoPE) decomposes exactly into a sum over its 2D-rotation frequency pairs, and each pair's wavelength limits how far it can discriminate position. Aligned with this structure, we propose the per-RoPE-wavelength distance window: it prunes the query--key inner-product terms beyond a wavelength-proportional distance. Unlike a sliding window, every key remains reachable, at least through the low-frequency pairs. The reduction rate is input-independent, with a closed form logarithmic in the sequence length $N$, in contrast to dynamic-sparse methods like MInference. Such token-level selection is orthogonal to our frequency-level pruning. The window can therefore be applied on top of those methods. On Qwen2.5-0.5B and Llama-3.2-3B, the window prunes 37--48\% of the query--key inner-product terms within each model's native context length. Relative to full attention, the top-1 match rate stays at 96--98\% and the mean output-distribution KL at the $10^{-3}$-nat level on LongBench-v2 contexts. We examine absolute scores on long-context benchmarks such as RULER, OpenAI-MRCR, LongCodeQA, and $\infty$Bench: they are broadly preserved. We implement the window as a slice of the query--key contraction axis, leaving the online-softmax recurrences untouched, and port it with minimal diffs into the released FlashAttention-4 prefill and FlashInfer decode. On RTX PRO 6000 with Llama, both ports outpace stock with gains growing with context length, up to $1.29\times$ at 128K. End to end on Qwen2.5-7B-1M, with 57\% of the inner-product terms pruned, the speedup reaches $1.31\times$ at a 1M-token context.

</details>


### [160] [Rubrics as Privileged Information for Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02948)
*Deepika Bablani,Ajay Gupta,Wanming Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将on-policy自蒸馏（OPSD）从可验证领域扩展到开放式生成任务，使用软特权信息（PI）——即rubric（评分细则）代替硬标签/参考答案作为蒸馏信号。在HealthBench和ResearchQA基准上，rubric作为PI（RuPI）显著优于rubric作为奖励的强化学习（RaR RL）和参考答案蒸馏，最高绝对分数提升+0.10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD在数学等可验证领域已展示出潜力（硬PI如标准答案结构性约束有效续接），但开放式生成中不存在单一正确答案，硬PI（参考完成）会过度约束学生模型。Rubric虽已被用作RL的标量奖励，但其作为稠密蒸馏信号的价值尚未被探索。作者旨在证明rubric蕴含的偏好结构比参考完成提供更丰富且更有效的训练信号。

Method: 将rubric作为软PI用于on-policy自蒸馏：同一模型在不同上下文下分别扮演教师和学生角色。具体地，蒸馏损失基于rubric引导的偏好信号作用在学生roll-out上（而非参考完成上）。对比了三种范式：(1) RuPI（rubric作为PI蒸馏）；(2) RaR RL（rubric作为标量奖励的强化学习）；(3) 参考完成PI（硬PI蒸馏）。在Qwen和Llama模型族上评估，使用HealthBench（开放式健康回答按医师rubric评分）和RubricHub Science语料上训练的ResearchQA评估。

Result: (1) RuPI在HealthBench上比RaR RL最高提升+0.10绝对分数；(2) 在匹配配方和KL方向下，RuPI比参考完成PI提升+0.034至+0.079绝对分数（跨三个模型）；(3) RuPI在ResearchQA上优于参考PI蒸馏和RaR RL（66.6% vs 64.2% vs 57.6%）；(4) 反直觉发现：软rubric PI在学生roll-out上比硬参考完成PI提供更大更有效的训练信号。

Conclusion: Rubric作为软PI进行蒸馏是开放式生成任务中一种优于rubric作为奖励的RL及硬参考PI蒸馏的有效范式。核心洞见是：参考完成只是有效回答集合中的一个点，向其蒸馏会过度约束学生；而rubric指定了有效回答集合共享的偏好结构，提供了token级稠密且灵活的训练信号。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD), where a single model acts as both student and teacher with different contexts, has shown promise in verifiable domains like math, where hard privileged information (PI) in the form of ground-truth answers structurally constrains valid continuations. We extend OPSD to open-ended generation using soft PI in the form of rubrics that guide preferences but admit many valid responses. Rubrics have served as scalar rewards for reinforcement learning (RL); we show that they provide substantially richer signal as dense PI for distillation, and contrary to intuition, soft rubric PI provides a larger and more effective training signal on student roll-outs than hard reference completion PI in this regime. A reference completion is one point in a set of valid responses, so distilling towards it over-constrains the student, while rubrics specify the preference structure shared across the set of valid responses. We show the effectiveness of using rubrics as PI for open-ended generation across Qwen and Llama model families and show that it outperforms rubric-as-reward (RaR) RL using HealthBench, a benchmark that grades open-ended health responses against physician-created rubrics, providing dense token-level supervision for open-ended tasks; RuPI beats RaR RL by up to +0.10 absolute score and, under matched recipe and KL direction, beats reference-PI by +0.034 to +0.079 absolute score across three models. We further show that these findings generalize to training on the RubricHub Science corpus and evaluating on ResearchQA: soft rubric PI outperforms both reference-PI distillation and RaR RL (66.6% vs. 64.2% and 57.6%).

</details>


### [161] [Schedule-Informed Temporal Fusion Forecasting of Hourly Airport Security-Checkpoint Throughput](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02950)
*Yinxiao Zhang,Sen Wang,Yi Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个将航班时刻表转换为安检吞吐量预报信号的框架，利用截断泊松核将座位容量分配至起飞前各小时，再通过Temporal Fusion Transformer结合历史吞吐量、航班活动和时间变量进行预测，在亚特兰大机场六小时直接预测中达到9.33%的加权平均绝对百分比误差，优于RNN（12.16%）和LSTM（11.37%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安检人员排班需要准确预测安检需求何时出现，但航班时刻表只记录起飞时间而非旅客到达安检点的时间，二者存在时间错位。现有方法缺乏将航班时刻表转化为旅客安检到达信号的机制，导致预测精度不足，影响安检资源调配效率。

Method: 首先利用2023-2024年TSA吞吐量数据和Cirium Diio航班时刻表，将国内和国际座位容量通过截断泊松核分布到起飞前各小时，生成与时间对齐的安检到达强度信号。然后使用Temporal Fusion Transformer将这些信号与历史吞吐量、计划航班活动和时间变量结合进行建模。训练按时间顺序进行，以2024年7-12月为测试集，采用五个随机种子，与RNN和LSTM基线模型对比评估。

Result: 在六小时直接预测中，提出的TFT模型达到9.33%的加权MAPE，显著优于RNN（12.16%）和LSTM（11.37%），且在高峰期误差最低。在六小时递归更新下，24-96小时预测范围内的误差保持在10.60%-11.04%之间，但更长的预测视界包含的有效预测起点较少。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将航班时刻表转化为可解释的起飞前安检负载信号，无需旅客-航班匹配即可支持安检人员提前排班、通道开放和跨天规划。由于观测到的吞吐量反映的是实际处理量而非无约束到达量，预测结果需结合当地人员配置、容量、排队和等待时间信息综合解读。

Abstract: Checkpoint staffing requires accurate forecasts of when screening demand will occur, yet flight schedules record departure times rather than passenger arrival times at security checkpoints. This study develops a framework that converts known flight schedules into temporally aligned signals for forecasting hourly checkpoint throughput. Using 2023-2024 Transportation Security Administration throughput data and Cirium Diio flight schedules for Hartsfield-Jackson Atlanta International Airport, domestic and international seat capacity was distributed across pre-departure hours using truncated Poisson kernels. A Temporal Fusion Transformer then combined these schedule-derived arrival-intensity signals with historical throughput, scheduled activity, and temporal variables. Models were trained chronologically, with July-December 2024 reserved for testing, and evaluated against recurrent neural network and long short-term memory models across five random seeds. For direct six-hour forecasts, the proposed model achieved a weighted mean absolute percentage error of 9.33%, compared with 12.16% for the recurrent neural network and 11.37% for long short-term memory, while also producing the lowest errors during peak periods. With six-hour recursive updates, errors remained between 10.60% and 11.04% across 24-96 hour horizons, although longer horizons contained fewer valid forecast origins. By transforming scheduled departures into interpretable pre-departure screening-load signals without requiring passenger-flight matching, the framework supports advance staffing, lane-opening, and multiday checkpoint planning. Because observed throughput reflects realized processing rather than unconstrained arrivals, the forecasts should be interpreted together with local staffing, capacity, queue, and wait-time information.

</details>


### [162] [SP3O: Reinforcement Learning from Segment Preferences without Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02951)
*Evan Assmus,Qining Zhang,Lei Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SP3O算法，一种无需奖励模型和评论家（critic-free）的、基于梯度的偏好强化学习（PbRL）算法，专为分段（segment）偏好反馈设计。SP3O通过离策略重要性采样构建策略价值差异估计器，并利用PPO型损失函数计算策略梯度，在机器人控制和LLM微调任务中表现出优于现有方法的性能，尤其在长时域任务中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好强化学习方法存在多方面不足：无需奖励模型的方法要么局限于bandit或确定性MDP（如DPO、P3O），要么使用零阶无梯度优化导致收敛速度较慢；此外，现有无奖励模型的PbRL算法几乎都使用轨迹级反馈，当轨迹较长时对人类评估者造成显著负担。分段反馈比轨迹反馈更短、更易于比较和评估，因此需要一种兼容分段偏好且基于梯度的无奖励模型算法。

Method: SP3O算法包含三个核心组成部分：(1) 利用分段级偏好反馈，通过离策略重要性采样构建精确的策略价值差异估计器；(2) 使用该估计器通过PPO型损失函数计算策略梯度，无需训练奖励模型，也无需评论家网络；(3) 提供理论基础，并分析分段长度选择的权衡。

Result: 在机器人控制和LLM微调场景下，SP3O与其他PbRL/RLHF算法相比展现出更优的性能，尤其在长时域任务中表现突出。

Conclusion: SP3O填补了无奖励模型、无评论家、基于梯度的偏好RL算法在分段反馈设置下的空白，兼具理论保证和实践优越性，为长时域任务中的偏好学习提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Preference-based reinforcement learning (PbRL) for general stochastic MDPs often requires training a reward model. Existing reward-model-free methods are either restricted to bandits or deterministic MDPs, such as DPO or P3O, or use zeroth-order, gradient-free optimization, which in general exhibits a slower convergence rate than gradient-based algorithms. Furthermore, existing reward-model-free preference-based RL algorithms almost exclusively use trajectory-level feedback, which can require significant effort from a human evaluator when trajectories are long. On the other hand, segments are much shorter, so they are easier to compare and evaluate. In this paper, we introduce a novel reward-model-free, critic-free, and gradient-based PbRL algorithm compatible with segment preferences named Segment Pairwise Proximal Policy Optimization (SP3O). SP3O utilizes segment-level preference feedback to construct an accurate policy value difference estimator via off-policy importance sampling, and then uses the estimator to compute the policy gradient via a PPO-type loss function. We provide a theoretical basis for the algorithm and analyze the tradeoff in choosing the segment length. We also evaluate it experimentally against other PbRL/RLHF algorithms in robotic control and LLM finetuning settings to show its improved performance, especially in long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [163] [Inverted Detection and Control in Steering Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02957)
*Max Torop,Aria Masoomi,Jennifer Dy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种"倒置检测-控制现象"：具有高度判别力的转向向量（SVs）在某些情况下会持续促进相反行为，称为倒置转向向量（ISVs）。作者提供了ISV效应的几何表征，并提出了一种无需生成或响应评分即可区分ISV的方法，通过定向符号翻转改进了基于检测的转向流水线（ITI），在27/30个实验中取得提升（+0.9%至+138%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转向向量（SVs）被广泛用于控制大语言模型输出的概念表达，其核心假设是SV相对概念具有线性判别性——展示该概念的文本表示应与SV对齐。然而作者发现有些高判别性的SV虽然与正例表示对齐，却始终促进相反行为，这违反了SV的基本假设，需要对其机理进行解释并提供纠偏方法。

Method: 首先识别并定义了倒置转向向量（ISVs）现象；其次对ISV的效应进行几何表征，分析发现沿这些方向进行转向会在判别性下游头中系统性地将表示推向"概念缺失"的状态（即使在解码前）。基于此分析，提出了一种无需生成文本或评估响应即可区分ISV的方法，进而进行有针对性的符号翻转，并将其应用于基于推断时干预（ITI）的检测式转向流水线以改善性能。

Result: 提出的方法在27/30个实验中取得改进，性能提升幅度从+0.9%到+138%。实验在Gemma 3 12B、Qwen 2.5 14B和Olmo 3 7B三个模型上跨5个概念进行评估。

Conclusion: 本文揭示并形式化了转向向量存在的倒置检测-控制现象（ISV），提供了几何层面的机理分析，并提出了一种无需生成的ISV识别与符号翻转方法，能够显著提升基于检测的转向流水线（如ITI）的效能，表明对ISV的感知和处理是提高概念控制可靠性的关键。

Abstract: Steering vectors (SVs) are widely used to influence the expression of concepts (e.g., truthfulness) in large language model outputs. A key assumption underpinning SVs is that they are linearly discriminative with respect to the concept: representations of texts that exhibit the concept are more aligned with the SV than those that do not, motivating shifts along the positive or negative SV direction to respectively promote or suppress the concept. In this work, we identify an inverted detection-control phenomenon in which some highly discriminative SVs that are aligned with positive representations can consistently promote the opposite behavior. We refer to such vectors as inverted-steering vectors (ISVs). We provide a geometric characterization of ISVs' effects, finding that steering along these directions systematically pushes representations in discriminative downstream heads as if the concept were absent, even prior to decoding. Motivated by this analysis, we propose an approach for distinguishing ISVs without requiring generation or associated response scoring. This enables targeted sign flips, which we use to improve a foundational detection-based steering pipeline via Inference Time Intervention (ITI). Our approach improves results in 27/30 experiments, ranging from +0.9% to +138%. We evaluate our findings on Gemma 3 12B, Qwen 2.5 14B, and Olmo 3 7B across 5 concepts.

</details>


### [164] [Scaling an Autoregressive Transformer for Single-Cell Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02961)
*Aleksandr Sharipov,Yusif Mukhtarov,Igor Molybog*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究单细胞基因表达向量的自监督生成任务，采用因果Transformer与量化VAE分词器相结合的架构，通过交叉熵损失进行训练。通过条件生成评估生物学保真度，并首次为单细胞基础模型拟合了双指数缩放定律和计算最优前沿，同时探讨了模型在扰动响应预测中的微调潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞基因表达数据的生成建模是一个重要且有挑战性的任务。现有工作缺乏对生成生物学保真度与模型缩放行为的系统表征。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究该任务的架构设计、缩放规律及下游应用潜力，从而为单细胞基础模型的理论理解和实践部署提供依据。

Method: 模型采用因果Transformer配合学习得到的量化VAE分词器，以交叉熵损失进行训练。模型以给定细胞类型的基因表达向量为条件，生成该细胞类型的额外基因表达向量。评估时比较生成分布与真实分布的一致性。通过变化参数量和训练数据量研究缩放性质，拟合双指数缩放定律并计算计算最优前沿。

Result: 作者首次为单细胞基础模型拟合出联合的双指数缩放定律，并获得了计算最优前沿曲线。模型生成的基因表达向量在保留细胞类型分布特性的同时展现了生物学保真度。这些结果为理解模型规模与数据量对单细胞生成质量的影响提供了量化认识。

Conclusion: 该工作证明所提出的因果Transformer+量化VAE架构能在单细胞基因表达生成任务上实现可靠的生物学保真度，且其缩放行为符合双指数规律。这为构建更大规模、更高效率的单细胞基础模型提供了理论指导，并展望了将其微调用于扰动响应预测的前景。

Abstract: We study a self-supervised generation task for single-cell gene expression vectors: given a set of vectors from a cell type, we aim to generate additional gene expression vectors of that cell type. For this task we characterize both the biological fidelity of the generated gene expression vectors and the scaling behavior of the pretraining loss. The model is a causal transformer paired with a learned quantized VAE tokenizer, trained with a cross-entropy loss. To evaluate the model, we condition it on held-out gene expression vectors of a cell type and generate vectors of gene expression, comparing the resulting distribution over gene expression vectors to the ground truth distribution of that cell type. We study the scaling properties of the proposed architecture by varying the number of trained parameters and the amount of training data. To our knowledge, we find the first jointly-fit two-exponent scaling law and compute-optimal frontier for a single-cell foundation model. Finally, we discuss how this pretrained model could be finetuned for perturbation response prediction.

</details>


### [165] [A Physics-Informed Hybrid Neural Operator for Transient Magnetization Prediction in Power Magnetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02965)
*Yachao Zhu,Qiujie Huang,Sinan Li,Yang Li,Gang Lei,Jianguo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG
